Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any extremal module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 827. The projective resolution has length at most 852 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by extremal generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 827, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 852. QED.
